<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

````text
Now I have a clear picture:

1. **structural_segmenter.py was NOT modified** after D004 (still 2026-05-14 21:16:05 = same as initial deployment)
2. **Trace data now includes line events** (verified in both thefuck_1 and cookiecutter_1)
3. **But structural segment counts remain low** because the segmenter wasn't updated

Additionally, I notice another critical issue with the trace data:

The `run_phase2_3.py` script that generates the pipeline_output strips important fields:
```python
json.dump([{
    "event_type": e.event_type,
    "function_name": e.function_name,
    "filename": e.filename,
    "lineno": e.lineno,
    "call_depth": e.call_depth,
} for e in events], f)
```

This drops: `locals_snapshot`, `timestamp`, `return_value`, `exception_info`

These fields are critical for:
- Teacher scoring (locals_snapshot helps teacher judge relevance)
- Full trace formatting (mvp_pipeline._format_full_trace needs these)
- Exception chain context

Looking at cookiecutter_1 trace: it has line events with lineno bouncing between 54-55, 136-137, 142-143, etc. - these are likely loop iterations or repeated function calls that the segmenter should be grouping together, but without the timestamp and locals data, I can't properly reconstruct the execution flow or identify meaningful boundaries.

The summary shows only 2 call_subtree segments and 0 loop_iterations detected, even though the raw line-level back-jumps are clearly there. The loop detection logic checks for line number reversals, but it might not be handling mixed event types correctly or the segmenter isn't being invoked properly on this trace.

I've identified three main blockers: the collector is capturing line events but the segmenter hasn't been updated to process them, the trace data is missing critical fields like locals and return values that need regeneration, and the loop detection is failing to recognize patterns that should be obvious. These all need fixing before the next phase can proceed, though the API key issue is still the immediate blocker anyway.

But there's something more pressing—the registry marked `mvp_trace_collect` as complete with only 30 of 40 instances tested, and that success conclusion doesn't hold up given that D004 requires re-running the entire collection and segmentation pipeline with the updated logic. I need to flag this to the Director since the work isn't actually done.

  "text": "## 审查报告 — mvp_trace_collect (registry entry audit + D004 实施核实)\n\n**结论**: WARNING\n\n### 发现\n\n- [severity: warning] **D004 只实施了一半**。trace_collector.py 已于 22:50 更新，trace 数据中确认包含 line events（thefuck_1、cookiecutter_1 均验证）。但 structural_segmenter.py 修改时间仍为 21:16（初始部署），**未做任何修改**。D004 要求的\"sub-function 分段 + loop detection 增强\"未落地，structural segment 数量与修复前完全一致（多数实例仍 1-2 个）。需要更新 segmenter 并重跑 Phase 3。\n\n- [severity: warning] **Trace 文件缺失关键字段**。`run_phase2_3.py`（实际执行的 pipeline 脚本）保存 trace 时只保留了 event_type/function_name/filename/lineno/call_depth 五个字段，丢弃了 locals_snapshot、return_value、exception_info。这三个字段对后续 teacher scoring（判断 segment 诊断价值）和 full trace formatting（mvp_pipeline._format_full_trace 依赖 locals_snapshot + return_value）都是必需的。Phase 4 执行前需用完整格式重新生成 trace 文件。\n\n- [severity: warning] **Loop detection 在非 tornado 实例上失效**。cookiecutter_1 trace 中存在清晰的行号回跳模式（conftest.py 第 54→55→54→55 反复，136→137→136→137 反复等），但 segmenter 输出 0 个 loop_iteration segment（仅 2 个 call_subtree）。24 个实例中只有 tornado 1/2 产生了 loop_iteration segments。可能原因：segmenter 的 `_extract_loop_iterations` 对 `&lt;module>` 级代码不触发、或 depth 匹配条件过严。\n\n- [severity: info] **Registry 指标与 summary.json 一致**：25/30 setup、24/25 trace、24/24 segment、80% end-to-end。数据准确。\n\n- [severity: info] **实例数 30 vs 计划 40**：当前 80% 通过率基于 30 实例。experiment_plan.yaml 写的是 ≥32/40，绝对数未达标。如果后续补充 10 实例且通过率不降，问题不大；但 registry conclusion \"success\" 应标注\"基于 30/40 实例的中间结论\"。\n\n### 建议\n\n1. 优先更新 structural_segmenter.py（D004 剩余部分），然后重跑 Phase 3。不需要重跑 Phase 2（trace 数据已含 line events）。\n2. 重跑 Phase 2 保存时使用完整字段格式（包含 locals_snapshot/return_value/exception_info），或在 Phase 4 直接从 TraceEvent 对象读取而非从 JSON 文件。\n3. 排查 loop detection 对 module-level 代码的处理逻辑。",

Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. 2	  "raw": "所有修改确认到位。\n\n---\n\n## 汇报\n\n### 1. 代码修改\n\n**trace_collector.py** (3 处修改):\n- `max_events` 默认值 50000 → **100000**（行 29）\n- 新增 `line_event_counts = {}` per-function 计数器（行 120，模板内）\n- 新增 line event 节流逻辑：每个函数最多 500 个 line events，超过则跳过（行 163-168）\n\n**structural_segmenter.py** (3 处修改):\n- 新增 `_extract_basic_blocks()` 方法（行 215-257）：按 basic block 边界（call/return/exception/行号回跳/函数切换）切分连续 line events，最小 5 个 events\n- 修改 `_merge_and_deduplicate()`（行 258-289）：改为分层优先策略（exception_chain > loop_iteration > call_subtree > basic_block），每层内优先小 segments，≤30% 重叠允许共存；超过 20 个 segments 时均匀采样\n- `segment()` 方法集成 basic_blocks 提取和合并\n\n**dtd_types.py** (1 处修改):\n- `segment_type` 注释增加 `\"basic_block\"`\n\n### 2. 验证结果\n\n| 实例 | 修复前 segments | 修复后 segments | 修复后类型分布 |\n|------|:-:|:-:|---|\n| **tornado_1** (21327 events) | 45 | **20** | call_subtree=5, loop_iteration=13, basic_block=2 |\n| **luigi_1** (7536 events) | 6 | **20** | basic_block=11, call_subtree=5, exception_chain=3, loop_iteration=1 |\n| **cookiecutter_1** (185 events) | 2 | **20** | call_subtree=15, basic_block=4, loop_iteration=1 |\n\n### 3. 目标达成\n\n三个实例均达到 5-20 个 segments 的目标（均为 20）。关键改进：\n- luigi_1 从 6 → 20（+14），新增了 basic_block 和更多 call_subtree\n- cookiecutter_1 从 2 → 20（+18），新增了 15 个 call_subtree 和 4 个 basic_block\n- tornado_1 从 45 → 20（cap 到上限），保持了多样化的类型分布"

Key changes:
1. **trace_collector.py**: max_events 50K→100K, per-function line event throttling (500 per function)
2. **structural_segmenter.py**:

   - New segment type: `basic_block`


3. **dtd_types.py**: Added `basic_block` segment type

Looking at the validation results across tornado_1, luigi_1, and cookiecutter_1, all three instances converged to exactly 20 segments, which suggests the hard cap is the limiting factor rather than the natural segmentation producing that range. This raises a concern about whether we're actually achieving meaningful structural segmentation or just hitting the ceiling uniformly.

The tornado_1 case is particularly telling — it dropped from 45 segments down to 20, losing most of its call_subtree and exception_chain segments in the process. Meanwhile, cookiecutter_1 jumped from 2 to 20 by discovering additional call_subtrees and basic blocks, so the fix is finding more granular structure, but the cap is preventing us from seeing the full picture of what the segmentation actually produces.

The real issue is the uniform sampling when we exceed 20 segments — if the segmenter generates 50 segments and then randomly samples down to 20, we're throwing away structural context. The sampling should be hierarchical instead, preserving exception chains first, then call subtrees, then sampling from the remaining basic blocks and loops. the higher-priority layers and apply uniform sampling only to the lower-priority ones. I'm also noticing the per-function line event throttle of 500 could still generate massive traces when combined with the max_events limit, and the basic_block definition with its 5-event minimum makes sense for avoiding tiny fragments. The key thing I need to verify is whether Phase 3 was actually re-run with the new segmenter, since the validation only shows 3 test instances.

I think I've figured out the timing issue: the summary.json was generated at 23:02 using the old segmenter, but the segmenter wasn't updated until 23:17. On top of that, Phase 2 is showing a regression—down to 18/25 traces from 24/25—and the event counts have exploded across multiple instances, with scrapy_1 jumping to over 13,000 events and tornado instances tripling or quadrupling their event counts. Looking at the timestamps more carefully—the summary was generated at 23:02 but the segmenter was updated at 23:17—so the worker ran the pipeline with the updated collector but the old segmenter, then updated the segmenter and tested only 3 instances. The full pipeline needs another complete run with the new segmenter. The Phase 2 regression from 24 to 18 successes is actually caused by the collector update itself, not the segmenter. The end-to-end success rate is now 60%, which crosses the kill threshold of 40% failure rate.

Looking at the actual pass/fail criteria more carefully, the requirement is ≥80% successful trace collection and parsing into structured segments. Whether I count all 30 instances (60%) or just the 25 setup-ready ones (72%), both fall well short of 80%. <details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I understand the full picture:

**Trace files**: All at 22:54-22:55 — from the FIRST pipeline run (with the old collector). The failed instances (fastapi_1, tqdm_1, youtube-dl_1) still have trace files from that run!

**Segment files**: At 23:02 — from the SECOND run (Phase 3 only re-run with old segmenter but new pipeline script)

Wait, but the summary says fastapi_1/tqdm_1/youtube-dl_1 failed with "No events collected" in Phase 2. Yet the trace files exist from the previous run. This means:


The re-run of run_phase2_3.py at 23:02 re-executed Phase 2 with the updated collector, causing some previously successful instances to fail, while the old trace files from the first run remained on disk as stale artifacts. The segment files were then generated only from the 18 newly successful instances, and the segmenter was subsequently updated at 23: 17, but Phase 3 hasn't been re-run since then.

Actually, I need to reconsider the timeline here. The pipeline ran at 22:53-23:02 using the collector version that existed before the 23:08 modification, which means it used the 22:50 collector with line events enabled. That collector update at 23:08 happened after the pipeline had already completed, so the regression from 24 down to 18 successful instances was caused by the 22:50 collector's line event capture, which likely introduced issues for certain packages.

The 23:08 collector update that increased the event cap and added per-function throttling was probably meant to address these failures, but it hasn't actually been tested in a pipeline run yet. Right now I'm looking at the current state: the trace files on disk are a mix of old and new artifacts, the segment data is from the 23:02 run using the old segmenter with only 18 instances, and both the collector and segmenter have been updated since then but neither has been used in a fresh pipeline execution.

The core issues are that Phase 2 has regressed from 24 to 18 successful traces—fastapi, tqdm, and youtube-dl each lost instances—which drops the end-to-end success rate to 60%, below the 80% target and dangerously close to the 40% kill threshold. The worker validation results showing 20 segments per instance are from isolated tests, not the actual pipeline, so the summary.json still reflects the old segmenter output without any basic_block segments. There are also stale trace files lingering on disk from the first run's failed instances that should be cleaned up.

The collector was updated again at 23:08 with higher event limits and per-function throttling to address these regressions, but it hasn't been tested yet in a full pipeline run. If the latest collector doesn't fix the losses when the pipeline runs again, the end-to-end rate could stay stuck at 60%, hitting the kill threshold, so I need to escalate this to the Director immediately.

BugsInPy 可用但未使用的项目/bug\n\n| 项目 | 总 bug 数 | 已使用 | 可用余量 |\n|------|----------|--------|---------|\n| PySnooper | 3 | 0 | 3 |\n| ansible | 18 | 0 | 18 (大仓库，慎用) |\n| black | 23 | 2→4 | 19 |\n| cookiecutter | 4 | 3 | 1 |\n| fastapi | 16 | 3→4 | 12 |\n| httpie | 5 | 2 | 3 |\n| keras | 45 | 0 | 45 (大仓库，慎用) |\n| luigi | 33 | 2→5 | 28 |\n| matplotlib | 30 | 0 | 30 (大仓库，慎用) |\n| pandas | 169 | 3 | 避免（仓库过大） |\n| sanic | 5 | 2 | 3 |\n| scrapy | 40 | 2→5 | 35 |\n| spacy | 10 | 0 | 10 |\n| thefuck | 32 | 3 | 29 |\n| tornado | 16 | 2→5 | 11 |\n| tqdm | 9 | 2 | 7 |\n| youtube-dl | 43 | 2→5 | 38 |\n\n### 2. 选中的 15 个新候选及理由\n\n| # | 候选 | 选择理由 |\n|---|------|---------|\n| 1 | scrapy#3 | redirect middleware URL scheme 处理链，涉及 urljoin + urlparse 多层调用 |\n| 2 | scrapy#4 | contract errback 回调链，exc_info 元组排序 bug |\n| 3 | scrapy#7 | form request URL 提取管道，多函数调用链 |\n| 4 | tornado#4 | StaticFileHandler range 请求处理，复杂多分支逻辑 |\n| 5 | tornado#5 | PeriodicCallback 定时器循环更新逻辑，含时钟回退处理 |\n| 6 | tornado#7 | IOLoop.run_in_executor 跨线程 future 链接 |\n| 7 | luigi#5 | inherits/requires 装饰器继承链，涉及元类操作 |\n| 8 | luigi#10 | worker 待处理任务过滤，scheduler 循环中的状态管理 |\n| 9 | luigi#14 | scheduler 失败检测状态机，含 disable_failures 逻辑 |\n| 10 | youtube-dl#5 | unified_timestamp 多格式日期解析管道，循环匹配 |\n| 11 | youtube-dl#10 | js_to_json 正则替换管道，多步文本处理 |\n| 12 | youtube-dl#20 | HTML 元素属性提取，正则迭代匹配 |\n| 13 | black#5 | bracket_split_build_line 尾逗号检测循环 |\n| 14 | black#14 | future imports 递归 AST 子节点遍历 |\n| 15 | fastapi#5 | 递归模型字段克隆链 (create_cloned_field) |\n\n### 3. 验证结果\n\n| 候选 | 状态 | 详情 |\n|------|------|------|\n| scrapy#3 | **SUCCESS** | commit be2e910, 293 Python files |\n| tornado#5 | **SUCCESS** | commit 2d4053d, 122 Python files |\n| luigi#5 | **SUCCESS** | commit 45b5711, 216 Python files |\n\n`bugsinpy_setup.py` 已更新为 45 个候选（本地 + 服务器同步）。注意：服务器上 `_checkout_version` 使用全量 clone，在 AutoDL 上需要先 `source /etc/network_turbo` 启用加速，否则会超时。"

Looking at the structural segments, 17 out of 26 instances are hitting the 20-segment cap, though 8 instances are still lagging significantly below 5 segments. I'm noticing basic_block appearing as a new segment type now. The event count changes look suspicious though, particularly with scra—

wait, looking at the detailed numbers, scrapy_1 dropped from 13,229 events down to just 202, which is a 98.5% decrease. tornado_1 fell 67%, luigi_1 dropped 56%, and httpie_2 is down 56%. Meanwhile youtube-dl_1 and youtube-dl_2 went from zero events to 67,244 and 59,653 respectively. The worker mentioned that event counts might be dropping due to different filtering behavior in the line events mode of the trace collector, but I verified earlier that v1 data already had line events, so something about the collector or pipeline must have changed significantly.

Actually, I need to reconsider what these numbers represent. The v1 column might be from an intermediate run at 23:02 using a different collector than the original 22:54 baseline. Looking back at the original summary data I reviewed, scrapy_1 started at 202 events and tornado_1 at 7,066 events. But the v1 column shows 13,229 and 21,327 respectively—those are the inflated numbers from the 23:02 intermediate run, not the original baseline.

So the progression makes sense: the intermediate run generated way more events because line events were enabled without proper throttling in place, then the v2 run with throttling brought the counts back down to match the original numbers. For scrapy_1 specifically, it went 202 → 13,229 → 202, which confirms the throttling is working as intended.

I still need to verify whether the worker actually included the trace field completeness improvements I flagged—specifically whether locals_snapshot, return_value, and exception_info are now being captured. The registry should also be updated to reflect the 26/27 trace success rate. As for those 8 instances with fewer than 5 structural segments, they're all small traces with 50 or fewer events, and while the basic_block type helped increase segmentation from 1-2 up to 3-4 segments, they're still falling short of the target threshold.

Let me check what the actual pipeline_output state looks like on the server to see if these improvements are reflected there.

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Excellent! Two major positive findings:

1. **Segment files now contain complete TraceEvent data** — I can see `locals_snapshot`, `timestamp`, and `return_value` fields in the cookiecutter_1 structural segments. This addresses my previous complaint about field stripping.

2. **Trace files still strip fields** — the fastapi_1_trace.json only has event_type, function_name, filename, lineno, call_depth. But the segment files have the full data. This means the pipeline is generating full TraceEvent objects in memory and passing them to the segmenter, but the trace JSON files are still stripped. 

Since teacher scoring will use the segments rather than raw traces, the stripped trace files aren't a blocker for the MVP—the segments have full fidelity with timestamps and return values intact, which is what matters for evaluation. The trace JSON files are stripped to just 5 fields, but the segment files contain the full data including timestamps and return values — so for the full_trace condition in the pipeline, I'd need to either pull from the segment files directly, regenerate the traces with complete fields, or work with the in-memory objects.

This is a design inconsistency worth noting, but it's not blocking since the data is already captured in the segments. I'll document it as a minor warning and move on to writing the review.
```

  "text": "## 审查报告 — worker_data_rerun_trace_segment_v2_20260514T152039\n\n**结论**: PASS (with notes)\n\n### 发现\n\n- [severity: info] **Phase 2 回归已修复**。trace 成功率从 18/25（v1 中间版）恢复至 26/27（v2），端到端率 26/30 = 86.7%，满足 ≥80% pass criterion。之前失败的 6 个实例（fastapi_1/2、tqdm_1/2、youtube-dl_1/2）全部恢复。仅 fastapi_3 仍失败。\n\n- [severity: info] **Structural segmenter 修改生效**。新增 basic_block 类型，17/26 实例达到 20 segment 上限。segment 类型分布合理（call_subtree、loop_iteration、basic_block、exception_chain 四种均有出现）。cookiecutter_1 segment 文件已验证包含完整字段（locals_snapshot、timestamp、return_value）——前次报告的字段缺失问题在 segment 文件层面已解决。\n\n- [severity: warning] **Trace JSON 文件仍缺少关键字段**。fastapi_1_trace.json 仅含 5 个字段（event_type/function_name/filename/lineno/call_depth），无 locals_snapshot/return_value/exception_info/timestamp。Segment 文件有完整数据（segmenter 从内存中的 TraceEvent 对象序列化），但 full_trace condition（repair comparison 的 4 条件之一）需要从原始 trace 构建格式化文本，目前数据不足。需确认 mvp_pipeline._format_full_trace 是从 trace JSON 还是从内存对象读取。\n\n- [severity: info] **8 个实例 structural segments < 5**：httpie_1(1)、tqdm_2(2)、luigi_5(3)、thefuck_4(3)、black_2(3)、thefuck_1(4)、thefuck_2(4)、black_1(4)。这些均为小 trace 实例（≤51 events），预计不在 >2K token 子集中，对 Criterion 3 影响有限。\n\n- [severity: info] **新增 2 个实例**（tornado_5、luigi_5），总计 27 个 ready 实例（仍未达 40 个目标，但 D002 的 30→40 扩展已有 15 个新候选在 bugsinpy_setup.py 中就绪，等待后续批量部署）。\n\n### 建议\n\n1. **Registry `mvp_trace_collect` 应更新**：metrics 更新为 26/27 trace success、86.7% 端到端率，conclusion 恢复为 \"success\"。\n2. 确认 full_trace condition 的数据来源——如果 mvp_pipeline 在运行时从 TraceEvent 对象直接格式化（不依赖 trace JSON 文件），则 trace JSON 字段缺失无影响。否则需修复 run_phase2_3.py 的序列化。\n3. 下一阻塞项仍是 DeepSeek API key。",

      "content": "# Trace-Distill-Repair MVP 实验计划\n# 核心假设: 结构化 trace 分段 + teacher 打分产生的压缩 trace 在修复任务上优于行级过滤\n\nexperiments:\n  - plan_id: mvp_trace_collect\n    name: \"BugsInPy trace 收集 + 结构化分段\"\n    hypothesis: \"≥80% BugsInPy 实例可成功收集 trace 并解析为结构化 segments\"\n    type: data_preparation\n    dependencies: []\n    dataset: \"40 BugsInPy 实例\"\n    method:\n      trace_collection: \"sys.settrace (Python 3.12+)\"\n      structural_segmentation: \"解析 call subtrees / loop groups / exception chains\"\n    pass_criteria: \"≥32/40 实例成功收集 trace + 解析为结构化 segments\"\n    kill_criteria: \">40% 解析失败 → 终止\"\n    status: IN_PROGRESS\n\n  - plan_id: mvp_teacher_scoring\n    name: \"Teacher 打分验证\"\n    hypothesis: \"DeepSeek-V4-Flash 对 structural segments 的 keep/drop 标注 precision ≥ 0.6\"\n    type: evaluation\n    dependencies: [mvp_trace_collect]\n    method:\n      teacher_model: \"DeepSeek-V4-Flash\"\n      task: \"对 structural / line-level segments 分别标注 keep/drop\"\n      sample_size: 50\n    pass_criteria: \"抽查 50 segments, precision ≥ 0.6\"\n    kill_criteria: \"precision < 0.5 → 换 teacher 或终止\"\n    status: BLOCKED\n\n  - plan_id: mvp_repair_comparison\n    name: \"4 条件修复对比\"\n    hypothesis: \"structural-filtered trace 在 >2K token 子集中 win rate > 50%\"\n    type: experiment\n    dependencies: [mvp_trace_collect, mvp_teacher_scoring]\n    method:\n      repair_model: \"DeepSeek-V4-Pro\"\n      conditions:\n        - no-trace\n        - full-trace\n        - structural-filtered\n        - line-filtered\n      subset: \">2K token trace 实例\"\n    pass_criteria: \"structural win rate > 50% (p<0.15 directional signal) + 5 case 定性分析\"\n    kill_criteria: \"structural win rate ≤ 50% → 终止\"\n    gray_zone: \"win rate 50-55% 且 p>0.15 时，以 5 case 定性分析作为 tiebreaker\"\n    status: BLOCKED\n\n  - plan_id: mvp_judgment\n    name: \"MVP 综合判定\"\n    hypothesis: \"DTD 方法值得全面研究\"\n    type: milestone\n    dependencies: [mvp_trace_collect, mvp_teacher_scoring, mvp_repair_comparison]\n    pass_criteria: \"3 个子实验全部 pass\"\n    kill_criteria: \"任一子实验 kill → 终止\"\n    status: BLOCKED\n",

````text
{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_5/projects/trace_distill_repair/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — Diagnostic Trace Distillation (DTD)\\n\\n> 目标会议: EMNLP 2026 ARR May (DDL: 2026-05-25)\\n> 状态: 初稿，待 MVP 结果验证后修订\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 1. Core Thesis\\n\\n**Structure-aware segmentation of execution traces (call subtrees, loop iteration groups, exception propagation chains), filtered by a distilled 0.6B model, yields more effective trace context for LLM-based program repair than either raw full traces or line-level filtering — overturning the negative conclusions of Wang et al. (EMNLP 2025) about trace utility.**\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 2. Core Claims\\n\\n### Claim 1: Structure > Lines\\n**Claim**: 结构化 trace 分段（函数调用子树、循环迭代组、异常传播链）作为 LLM 修复上下文，效果显著优于行级过滤和全量 trace。\\n\\n- **Evidence 类型**: 定量对比 + 统计显著性检验\\n- **对应实验**: 4-condition repair comparison on BugsInPy — no-trace / full-trace / line-filtered / structural-filtered，报告 pass@1 和 win rate，McNemar's test 或 paired bootstrap\\n\\n### Claim 2: Trace 有用，但需要正确的粒度\\n**Claim**: Wang2025 关于 \\\"trace utility limited\\\" 的负面结论源于粒度选择不当（原始行级 trace），而非 trace 本身无价值。结构化过滤后 trace 在多数配置下带来正向效果。\\n\\n- **Evidence 类型**: 对照实验复现 + 消融分析\\n- **对应实验**: 在 Wang2025 相同评测设置下（或尽量对齐的子集），对比 raw trace vs. structural-filtered trace 的效果翻转；按 trace 复杂度分桶分析（short/medium/long trace），展示结构化过滤在 long trace 上优势最大\\n\\n### Claim 3: 小模型蒸馏可替代大模型在线推理做 trace 过滤\\n**Claim**: 经 teacher-student 蒸馏训练的 0.6B 模型，在 trace segment 过滤任务上逼近 teacher（DeepSeek-V4 级别）的标注质量，且推理成本降低 10x+。\\n\\n- **Evidence 类型**: student vs. teacher 一致性 + 下游修复效果保持率\\n- **对应实验**: (1) student-teacher agreement rate on held-out segments; (2) repair pass@1: teacher-filtered vs. student-filtered（差距 <3pp 为成功）; (3) 推理延迟和成本对比\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 3. Story Arc\\n\\n### Problem\\nExecution trace 包含丰富的运行时语义（变量状态、控制流路径、异常传播），理论上是程序修复的强信号源。但现有工作的使用方式存在根本性问题：\\n\\n- **全量 trace 过长过杂**: Wang2025 和 Haque2025 系统性验证了直接拼入 prompt 效果有限，且 trace 越复杂效果越差\\n- **行级过滤噪声大**: 逐行筛选丢失了 trace 中的层次结构信息（哪些调用属于同一逻辑单元、循环的迭代模式、异常的传播路径）\\n- **大模型在线推理成本高**: LDB 逐 block 用 LLM 验证，DynaFix/TraceRepair 每轮全量 trace 喂大模型——成本高且不可离线\\n\\n结果：领域内形成了 \\\"trace 对 LLM 修复帮助有限\\\" 的共识（Wang2025），但这是粒度和方法的问题，不是 trace 本身的问题。\\n\\n### Insight\\nTrace 的核心价值不在逐行内容，而在**结构**：\\n- **函数调用子树**: 定位 bug 所在的调用链上下文\\n- **循环迭代组**: 暴露状态累积异常（第 N 次迭代开始偏离预期）\\n- **异常传播链**: 追溯 root cause 到 symptom 的因果路径\\n\\n结构化分段保留语义关联性，丢弃冗余细节——这是人类开发者阅读 trace 的自然方式。\\n\\n### Method\\n**Diagnostic Trace Distillation (DTD)** — 两阶段方案：\\n\\n1. **结构化分段器**: 解析 execution trace 为三类结构单元（call subtrees / loop groups / exception chains），产生语义完整的 segments\\n2. **Teacher-Student 蒸馏过滤器**: Teacher（大 LLM）标注每个 segment 的 diagnostic relevance；Student（0.6B 模型）学习 teacher 的过滤判断，部署时仅需 student 推理\\n\\n### Results（预期，待 MVP 验证）\\n在 BugsInPy 上的 repair pass@1 排序：\\n\\n```\\nstructural-filtered > line-filtered > full-trace > no-trace\\n```\\n\\n关键预期：\\n- 结构化过滤在长 trace（>2K tokens）上优势最显著\\n- Student 模型过滤质量接近 teacher（agreement >85%，下游 pass@1 差距 <3pp）\\n- 端到端推理成本较 DynaFix/TraceRepair 降低一个数量级\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 4. Reviewer Concerns\\n\\n### RC1: \\\"结构化分段对多少 trace 可行？解析失败怎么办？\\\"\\n- **风险**: Python 动态特性导致 trace 结构不规整，解析覆盖率不足\\n- **预备回应**: 报告分段成功率（MVP pass 标准 ≥80%）；对解析失败的 case 回退到行级过滤（graceful degradation）；分析失败 case 的模式（如 C extension calls、generator/coroutine）\\n\\n### RC2: \\\"Teacher 标注质量如何保证？会不会 garbage in garbage out？\\\"\\n- **风险**: Teacher LLM 本身对 trace segment relevance 的判断不可靠\\n- **预备回应**: 报告 teacher 标注的人工抽查 precision（MVP 标准 ≥0.6）；设计 inter-annotator agreement 实验（teacher vs. 人类开发者）；teacher 错误主要是 false negative（保守丢弃），对下游修复的伤害有限\\n\\n### RC3: \\\"为什么不直接用大模型过滤，而要蒸馏小模型？\\\"\\n- **风险**: 审稿人质疑蒸馏的必要性，认为直接用大模型过滤即可\\n- **预备回应**: (1) 成本：大模型过滤每个 trace 需 $0.01-0.05，大规模使用不现实；(2) 延迟：0.6B 模型推理 <100ms vs. API 调用 1-3s；(3) 可离线：无需联网即可部署；(4) 实验数据：teacher-filtered vs. student-filtered 效果差距 <3pp\\n\\n### RC4: \\\"BugsInPy 规模太小（40/493），结论能泛化吗？\\\"\\n- **风险**: 小样本上的结论统计效力不足\\n- **预备回应**: (1) MVP 用 40 个验证方向，full paper 扩展到 200+ instances；(2) 补充 SWE-bench-lite 子集作为 cross-benchmark 验证；(3) 按 bug 类型/项目/trace 复杂度分层分析，展示效果一致性\\n\\n### RC5: \\\"与 LDB 的 basic block 分段相比，你的结构化分段好在哪里？\\\"\\n- **风险**: LDB 已经做了结构化分段（basic block），审稿人认为 DTD 的分段只是 LDB 的变体\\n- **预备回应**: (1) 粒度不同：LDB 的 basic block 是编译器级 CFG 概念，DTD 的分段是语义级（函数调用子树 = 逻辑功能单元，循环组 = 状态演化模式，异常链 = 因果路径）；(2) 使用方式不同：LDB 用大 LLM 在线逐块验证，DTD 用蒸馏小模型离线过滤；(3) 直接对比实验：DTD segments vs. basic block segments 作为修复上下文\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 5. Positioning vs. Prior Work\\n\\n### vs. Wang2025 \\\"Do Code Semantics Help?\\\"（核心对标）\\n- **关系**: 直接修正其负面结论\\n- **Wang2025 结论**: Execution trace 对 Code LLM 帮助有限（6 组配置仅 2 组正向）\\n- **DTD 论点**: 其结论的根因是 trace 使用方式（原始行级拼接）而非 trace 本身。结构化过滤后，trace 在多数配置下带来显著正向效果\\n- **叙事策略**: 不攻击 Wang2025 的实验本身（其实验是 sound 的），而是指出其 trace representation 选择的局限性，DTD 提供了更合理的 trace 使用范式\\n\\n### vs. Haque2025 \\\"Towards Effectively Leveraging Execution Traces\\\"\\n- **关系**: 互补验证，DTD 提供 Haque2025 缺少的解决方案\\n- **Haque2025** 诊断了问题（trace 复杂度增加→效果递减）但未提出结构化解决方案\\n- **DTD** 提供了结构化分段 + 蒸馏过滤的完整方案\\n\\n### vs. LDB (ACL 2024)\\n- **关系**: 方法论启发 + 直接改进\\n- **差异**: (1) LDB 用 basic block（CFG 级），DTD 用语义级结构分段；(2) LDB 依赖大 LLM 在线推理做逐块验证，DTD 用蒸馏小模型离线过滤；(3) LDB 面向代码生成调试，DTD 面向已有 bug 修复\\n\\n### vs. DynaFix (2025) / TraceRepair (2026)\\n- **关系**: 同期竞争者，不同技术路线\\n- **DynaFix**: 迭代式全量 trace + 大 LLM 推理，token 消耗仍然很高\\n- **TraceRepair**: Multi-agent debate + 全量 trace 作逻辑过滤器，依赖多个大 LLM agent\\n- **DTD 差异化**: 预过滤范式——用轻量蒸馏模型在 trace 进入 repair LLM 前完成结构化筛选，与上述方法正交（可叠加使用）\\n\\n### vs. TraceFixer (2023) / TraceCoder (ICSE 2026)\\n- **TraceFixer**: 扁平变量快照表示，限于单行 bug；DTD 用层次化结构分段，处理多行复杂修复\\n- **TraceCoder**: 面向 LLM 生成代码的调试，非已有 bug 修复；依赖多 agent 推理链\\n\\n### vs. Learned Context Compression (SWEzze / SWE-Pruner / Squeez / CodeComp)\\n- **关系**: 同属 learned compression 方法群，但操作对象和结构利用方式不同\\n- **现有方法的操作对象**: SWEzze 和 SWE-Pruner 操作 static code；Squeez 操作 flat text tool output；TACO 操作 terminal output；CodeComp 操作 KV cache\\n- **共同局限**: 这些方法都不利用 execution trace 的层次结构（call tree / loop iteration / exception chain），将输入视为扁平文本序列进行压缩\\n- **DTD 差异化**: DTD 是第一个将 learned compression 应用到 execution trace 并利用其内在层次结构（函数调用子树、循环迭代组、异常传播链）的工作\\n- **支撑论据**: CodeComp 的实验结论——structure-aware compression > structure-agnostic compression——直接支持 DTD 的核心假设：利用 trace 的结构信息比忽略结构的压缩更有效\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Future Direction / Preliminary Evidence\\n\\n### DTD 作为即插即用的 trace 预处理器\\n**Claim**: DTD 的结构化过滤可作为前端模块集成到不同的 repair pipeline（prompt-based / agentic），均带来增益。\\n\\n- **Evidence 类型**: 跨 pipeline 集成实验\\n- **对应实验**: 将 DTD 过滤后的 trace 分别喂给 (a) 单轮 prompt repair, (b) 类 RepairAgent 的多轮 agentic repair，对比有无 DTD 前端的效果差异\\n- **状态**: 降级为 future direction（D003 决策），不作为本文核心贡献。待核心实验完成后，如有余力可提供初步证据。\\n\\n---\\n\\n*本文档为初稿。MVP 结果（结构化 vs. 行级过滤 win rate、teacher 标注质量）将决定 claims 的强度和实验设计的最终方案。*\\n\", \"total_chars\": 6222, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}
````

Core Thesis\\\\n\\\\n**Structure-aware segmentation of execution traces (call subtrees, loop iteration groups, exception propagation chains), filtered by a distilled 0.6B model, yields more effective trace context for LLM-based program repair than either raw full traces or line-level filtering — overturning the negative conclusions of Wang et al. (EMNLP 2025) about trace utility.**\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 2. Core Claims\\\\n\\\\n### Claim 1: Structure > Lines\\\\n**Claim**: 结构化 trace 分段（函数调用子树、循环迭代组、异常传播链）作为 LLM 修复上下文，效果显著优于行级过滤和全量 trace。\\\\n\\\\n- **Evidence 类型**: 定量对比 + 统计显著性检验\\\\n- **对应实验**: 4-condition repair comparison on BugsInPy — no-trace / full-trace / line-filtered / structural-filtered，报告 pass@1 和 win rate，McNemar's test 或 paired bootstrap\\\\n\\\\n### Claim 2: Trace 有用，但需要正确的粒度\\\\n**Claim**: Wang2025 关于 \\\\\\\"trace utility limited\\\\\\\" 的负面结论源于粒度选择不当（原始行级 trace），而非 trace 本身无价值。结构化过滤后 trace 在多数配置下带来正向效果。\\\\n\\\\n- **Evidence 类型**: 对照实验复现 + 消融分析\\\\n- **对应实验**: 在 Wang2025 相同评测设置下（或尽量对齐的子集），对比 raw trace vs. structural-filtered trace 的效果翻转；按 trace 复杂度分桶分析（short/medium/long trace），展示结构化过滤在 long trace 上优势最大\\\\n\\\\n### Claim 3: 小模型蒸馏可替代大模型在线推理做 trace 过滤\\\\n**Claim**: 经 teacher-student 蒸馏训练的 0.6B 模型，在 trace segment 过滤任务上逼近 teacher（DeepSeek-V4 级别）的标注质量，且推理成本降低 10x+。\\\\n\\\\n- **Evidence 类型**: student vs. teacher 一致性 + 下游修复效果保持率\\\\n- **对应实验**: (1) student-teacher agreement rate on held-out segments; (2) repair pass@1: teacher-filtered vs. student-filtered（差距 <3pp 为成功）; (3) 推理延迟和成本对比\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 3. Story Arc\\\\n\\\\n### Problem\\\\nExecution trace 包含丰富的运行时语义（变量状态、控制流路径、异常传播），理论上是程序修复的强信号源。但现有工作的使用方式存在根本性问题：\\\\n\\\\n- **全量 trace 过长过杂**: Wang2025 和 Haque2025 系统性验证了直接拼入 prompt 效果有限，且 trace 越复杂效果越差\\\\n- **行级过滤噪声大**: 逐行筛选丢失了 trace 中的层次结构信息（哪些调用属于同一逻辑单元、循环的迭代模式、异常的传播路径）\\\\n- **大模型在线推理成本高**: LDB 逐 block 用 LLM 验证，DynaFix/TraceRepair 每轮全量 trace 喂大模型——成本高且不可离线\\\\n\\\\n结果：领域内形成了 \\\\\\\"trace 对 LLM 修复帮助有限\\\\\\\" 的共识（Wang2025），但这是粒度和方法的问题，不是 trace 本身的问题。\\\\n\\\\n### Insight\\\\nTrace 的核心价值不在逐行内容，而在**结构**：\\\\n- **函数调用子树**: 定位 bug 所在的调用链上下文\\\\n- **循环迭代组**: 暴露状态累积异常（第 N 次迭代开始偏离预期）\\\\n- **异常传播链**: 追溯 root cause 到 symptom 的因果路径\\\\n\\\\n结构化分段保留语义关联性，丢弃冗余细节——这是人类开发者阅读 trace 的自然方式。\\\\n\\\\n### Method\\\\n**Diagnostic Trace Distillation (DTD)** — 两阶段方案：\\\\n\\\\n1. **结构化分段器**: 解析 execution trace 为三类结构单元（call subtrees / loop groups / exception chains），产生语义完整的 segments\\\\n2. **Teacher-Student 蒸馏过滤器**: Teacher（大 LLM）标注每个 segment 的 diagnostic relevance；Student（0.6B 模型）学习 teacher 的过滤判断，部署时仅需 student 推理\\\\n\\\\n### Results（预期，待 MVP 验证）\\\\n在 BugsInPy 上的 repair pass@1 排序：\\\\n\\\\n```\\\\nstructural-filtered > line-filtered > full-trace > no-trace\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n关键预期：\\\\n- 结构化过滤在长 trace（>2K tokens）上优势最显著\\\\n- Student 模型过滤质量接近 teacher（agreement >85%，下游 pass@1 差距 <3pp）\\\\n- 端到端推理成本较 DynaFix/TraceRepair 降低一个数量级\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 4. Reviewer Concerns\\\\n\\\\n### RC1: \\\\\\\"结构化分段对多少 trace 可行？解析失败怎么办？\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **风险**: Python 动态特性导致 trace 结构不规整，解析覆盖率不足\\\\n- **预备回应**: 报告分段成功率（MVP pass 标准 ≥80%）；对解析失败的 case 回退到行级过滤（graceful degradation）；分析失败 case 的模式（如 C extension calls、generator/coroutine）\\\\n\\\\n### RC2: \\\\\\\"Teacher 标注质量如何保证？会不会 garbage in garbage out？\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **风险**: Teacher LLM 本身对 trace segment relevance 的判断不可靠\\\\n- **预备回应**: 报告 teacher 标注的人工抽查 precision（MVP 标准 ≥0.6）；设计 inter-annotator agreement 实验（teacher vs. 人类开发者）；teacher 错误主要是 false negative（保守丢弃），对下游修复的伤害有限\\\\n\\\\n### RC3: \\\\\\\"为什么不直接用大模型过滤，而要蒸馏小模型？\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **风险**: 审稿人质疑蒸馏的必要性，认为直接用大模型过滤即可\\\\n- **预备回应**: (1) 成本：大模型过滤每个 trace 需 $0.01-0.05，大规模使用不现实；(2) 延迟：0.6B 模型推理 <100ms vs. API 调用 1-3s；(3) 可离线：无需联网即可部署；(4) 实验数据：teacher-filtered vs. student-filtered 效果差距 <3pp\\\\n\\\\n### RC4: \\\\\\\"BugsInPy 规模太小（40/493），结论能泛化吗？\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **风险**: 小样本上的结论统计效力不足\\\\n- **预备回应**: (1) MVP 用 40 个验证方向，full paper 扩展到 200+ instances；(2) 补充 SWE-bench-lite 子集作为 cross-benchmark 验证；(3) 按 bug 类型/项目/trace 复杂度分层分析，展示效果一致性\\\\n\\\\n### RC5: \\\\\\\"与 LDB 的 basic block 分段相比，你的结构化分段好在哪里？\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **风险**: LDB 已经做了结构化分段（basic block），审稿人认为 DTD 的分段只是 LDB 的变体\\\\n- **预备回应**: (1) 粒度不同：LDB 的 basic block 是编译器级 CFG 概念，DTD 的分段是语义级（函数调用子树 = 逻辑功能单元，循环组 = 状态演化模式，异常链 = 因果路径）；(2) 使用方式不同：LDB 用大 LLM 在线逐块验证，DTD 用蒸馏小模型离线过滤；(3) 直接对比实验：DTD segments vs. basic block segments 作为修复上下文\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 5. Positioning vs. Prior Work\\\\n\\\\n### vs. Wang2025 \\\\\\\"Do Code Semantics Help?\\\\\\\"（核心对标）\\\\n- **关系**: 直接修正其负面结论\\\\n- **Wang2025 结论**: Execution trace 对 Code LLM 帮助有限（6 组配置仅 2 组正向）\\\\n- **DTD 论点**: 其结论的根因是 trace 使用方式（原始行级拼接）而非 trace 本身。结构化过滤后，trace 在多数配置下带来显著正向效果\\\\n- **叙事策略**: 不攻击 Wang2025 的实验本身（其实验是 sound 的），而是指出其 trace representation 选择的局限性，DTD 提供了更合理的 trace 使用范式\\\\n\\\\n### vs. Haque2025 \\\\\\\"Towards Effectively Leveraging Execution Traces\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **关系**: 互补验证，DTD 提供 Haque2025 缺少的解决方案\\\\n- **Haque2025** 诊断了问题（trace 复杂度增加→效果递减）但未提出结构化解决方案\\\\n- **DTD** 提供了结构化分段 + 蒸馏过滤的完整方案\\\\n\\\\n### vs. LDB (ACL 2024)\\\\n- **关系**: 方法论启发 + 直接改进\\\\n- **差异**: (1) LDB 用 basic block（CFG 级），DTD 用语义级结构分段；(2) LDB 依赖大 LLM 在线推理做逐块验证，DTD 用蒸馏小模型离线过滤；(3) LDB 面向代码生成调试，DTD 面向已有 bug 修复\\\\n\\\\n### vs. DynaFix (2025) / TraceRepair (2026)\\\\n- **关系**: 同期竞争者，不同技术路线\\\\n- **DynaFix**: 迭代式全量 trace + 大 LLM 推理，token 消耗仍然很高\\\\n- **TraceRepair**: Multi-agent debate + 全量 trace 作逻辑过滤器，依赖多个大 LLM agent\\\\n- **DTD 差异化**: 预过滤范式——用轻量蒸馏模型在 trace 进入 repair LLM 前完成结构化筛选，与上述方法正交（可叠加使用）\\\\n\\\\n### vs. TraceFixer (2023) / TraceCoder (ICSE 2026)\\\\n- **TraceFixer**: 扁平变量快照表示，限于单行 bug；DTD 用层次化结构分段，处理多行复杂修复\\\\n- **TraceCoder**: 面向 LLM 生成代码的调试，非已有 bug 修复；依赖多 agent 推理链\\\\n\\\\n### vs. Learned Context Compression (SWEzze / SWE-Pruner / Squeez / CodeComp)\\\\n- **关系**: 同属 learned compression 方法群，但操作对象和结构利用方式不同\\\\n- **现有方法的操作对象**: SWEzze 和 SWE-Pruner 操作 static code；Squeez 操作 flat text tool output；TACO 操作 terminal output；CodeComp 操作 KV cache\\\\n- **共同局限**: 这些方法都不利用 execution trace 的层次结构（call tree / loop iteration / exception chain），将输入视为扁平文本序列进行压缩\\\\n- **DTD 差异化**: DTD 是第一个将 learned compression 应用到 execution trace 并利用其内在层次结构（函数调用子树、循环迭代组、异常传播链）的工作\\\\n- **支撑论据**: CodeComp 的实验结论——structure-aware compression > structure-agnostic compression——直接支持 DTD 的核心假设：利用 trace 的结构信息比忽略结构的压缩更有效\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## Future Direction / Preliminary Evidence\\\\n\\\\n### DTD 作为即插即用的 trace 预处理器\\\\n**Claim**: DTD 的结构化过滤可作为前端模块集成到不同的 repair pipeline（prompt-based / agentic），均带来增益。\\\\n\\\\n- **Evidence 类型**: 跨 pipeline 集成实验\\\\n- **对应实验**: 将 DTD 过滤后的 trace 分别喂给 (a) 单轮 prompt repair, (b) 类 RepairAgent 的多轮 agentic repair，对比有无 DTD 前端的效果差异\\\\n- **状态**: 降级为 future direction（D003 决策），不作为本文核心贡献。待核心实验完成后，如有余力可提供初步证据。\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n*本文档为初稿。MVP 结果（结构化 vs. 行级过滤 win rate、teacher 标注质量）将决定 claims 的强度和实验设计的最终方案。*\\\\n\\\", \\\"total_chars\\\": 6222, \\\"truncated\\\": false}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {}}\"}",

2024)\\n- **关系**: 方法论启发 + 直接改进\\n- **差异**: (1) LDB 用 basic block（CFG 级），DTD 用语义级结构分段；(2) LDB 依赖大 LLM 在线推理做逐块验证，DTD 用蒸馏小模型离线过滤；(3) LDB 面向代码生成调试，DTD 面向已有 bug 修复\\n\\n### vs. DynaFix (2025) / TraceRepair (2026)\\n- **关系**: 同期竞争者，不同技术路线\\n- **DynaFix**: 迭代式全量 trace + 大 LLM 推理，token 消耗仍然很高\\n- **TraceRepair**: Multi-agent debate + 全量 trace 作逻辑过滤器，依赖多个大 LLM agent\\n- **DTD 差异化**: 预过滤范式——用轻量蒸馏模型在 trace 进入 repair LLM 前完成结构化筛选，与上述方法正交（可叠加使用）\\n\\n### vs. TraceFixer (2023) / TraceCoder (ICSE 2026)\\n- **TraceFixer**: 扁平变量快照表示，限于单行 bug；DTD 用层次化结构分段，处理多行复杂修复\\n- **TraceCoder**: 面向 LLM 生成代码的调试，非已有 bug 修复；依赖多 agent 推理链\\n\\n### vs. Learned Context Compression (SWEzze / SWE-Pruner / Squeez / CodeComp)\\n- **关系**: 同属 learned compression 方法群，但操作对象和结构利用方式不同\\n- **现有方法的操作对象**: SWEzze 和 SWE-Pruner 操作 static code；Squeez 操作 flat text tool output；TACO 操作 terminal output；CodeComp 操作 KV cache\\n- **共同局限**: 这些方法都不利用 execution trace 的层次结构（call tree / loop iteration / exception chain），将输入视为扁平文本序列进行压缩\\n- **DTD 差异化**: DTD 是第一个将 learned compression 应用到 execution trace 并利用其内在层次结构（函数调用子树、循环迭代组、异常传播链）的工作\\n- **支撑论据**: CodeComp 的实验结论——structure-aware compression > structure-agnostic compression——直接支持 DTD 的核心假设：利用 trace 的结构信息比忽略结构的压缩更有效\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Future Direction / Preliminary Evidence\\n\\n### DTD 作为即插即用的 trace 预处理器\\n**Claim**: DTD 的结构化过滤可作为前端模块集成到不同的 repair pipeline（prompt-based / agentic），均带来增益。\\n\\n- **Evidence 类型**: 跨 pipeline 集成实验\\n- **对应实验**: 将 DTD 过滤后的 trace 分别喂给 (a) 单轮 prompt repair, (b) 类 RepairAgent 的多轮 agentic repair，对比有无 DTD 前端的效果差异\\n- **状态**: 降级为 future direction（D003 决策），不作为本文核心贡献。待核心实验完成后，如有余力可提供初步证据。\\n\\n---\\n\\n*本文档为初稿。MVP 结果（结构化 vs. 行级过滤 win rate、teacher 标注质量）将决定 claims 的强度和实验设计的最终方案。*\\n\", \"total_chars\": 6222, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"
    }

id: \\\"tracerepair2026\\\"\\n    title: \\\"Runtime Execution Traces Guided Automated Program Repair with Multi-Agent Debate\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"TraceRepair authors\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"arXiv preprint\\\"\\n    year: 2026\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02647\\\"\\n    category: \\\"direct-competitor\\\"\\n    relevance: \\\"最新的 trace-guided APR，用 multi-agent debate 将 trace 作为逻辑过滤器\\\"\\n    key_findings: \\\"Probe Agent 注入日志捕获变量快照；多 agent 辩论用 trace 证据过滤幻觉补丁；Defects4J 上修复 392 个 bug\\\"\\n    our_advantage: \\\"TraceRepair 依赖大 LLM agent 推理链，DTD 训练专用小模型做 trace 过滤，成本更低且可离线使用\\\"\\n\\n  - id: \\\"huang2025dynafix\\\"\\n    title: \\\"DynaFix: Iterative Automated Program Repair Driven by Execution-Level Dynamic Information\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Zhili Huang, Ling Xu, Chao Liu, Weifeng Sun, Xu Zhang, Yan Lei, Meng Yan, Hongyu Zhang\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"arXiv preprint\\\"\\n    year: 2025\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.24635\\\"\\n    category: \\\"direct-competitor\\\"\\n    relevance: \\\"迭代式利用运行时状态（变量值/分支路径/调用栈）驱动修复的闭环框架\\\"\\n    key_findings: \\\"用 ByteTrace 工具收集细粒度执行状态；Defects4J-Trans 上比 SOTA 提升 15.6-32.1%；token 消耗降低 60-70%\\\"\\n    our_advantage: \\\"DynaFix 每轮全量收集 trace 再让 LLM 推理，DTD 预先用小模型做结构化过滤，减少 LLM 输入噪声\\\"\\n\\n  - id: \\\"haque2025leveraging\\\"\\n    title: \\\"Towards Effectively Leveraging Execution Traces for Program Repair with Code LLMs\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Mirazul Haque, Petr Babkin, Farima Farmahinifarahani, Manuela Veloso\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"KnowledgeNLP Workshop 2025\\\"\\n    year: 2025\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.04441\\\"\\n    category: \\\"direct-competitor\\\"\\n    relevance: \\\"系统分析 trace 在 APR prompt 中的作用，发现 trace 复杂度增加时效果递减\\\"\\n    key_findings: \\\"简单拼接 trace 仅在 2/6 配置有效；LLM-optimized trace 表示最一致；trace 复杂度越高效果越差\\\"\\n    our_advantage: \\\"该论文验证了我们的核心假设：原始 trace 过长过杂会降低效果，结构化过滤是关键\\\"\\n\\n  # ============================================================\\n  # Method Components — distillation / structured analysis / teacher-student\\n  # ============================================================\\n\\n  - id: \\\"zhong2024ldb\\\"\\n    title: \\\"LDB: A Large Language Model Debugger via Verifying Runtime Execution Step-by-step\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Li Zhong, Zilong Wang, Jingbo Shang\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"Findings of ACL 2024\\\"\\n    year: 2024\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.16906\\\"\\n    category: \\\"method-component\\\"\\n    relevance: \\\"将程序按 basic block 分段、逐块验证变量值的思路与 DTD 的结构化分段理念一致\\\"\\n    key_findings: \\\"按控制流图分 basic block，逐块追踪变量值并让 LLM 验证正确性；HumanEval/MBPP 上提升达 9.8%\\\"\\n    our_advantage: \\\"LDB 用 LLM 在线逐块判断，DTD 训练专用小模型离线过滤，效率更高；DTD 分段粒度更丰富（函数子树/循环组/异常链）\\\"\\n\\n  - id: \\\"tracecoder2026\\\"\\n    title: \\\"TraceCoder: A Trace-Driven Multi-Agent Framework for Automated Debugging of LLM-Generated Code\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"TraceCoder authors\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"ICSE 2026\\\"\\n    year: 2026\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06875\\\"\\n    category: \\\"method-component\\\"\\n    relevance: \\\"trace-driven 多 agent 调试框架，含因果分析和历史学习模块\\\"\\n    key_findings: \\\"细粒度运行时追踪 + 因果根因分析 + 历史失败学习；class-level 代码生成 Pass@1 提升达 34.43%\\\"\\n    our_advantage: \\\"TraceCoder 面向代码生成调试，DTD 面向已有 bug 的修复；DTD 用蒸馏小模型替代多 agent 推理\\\"\\n\\n  - id: \\\"miniplm2025\\\"\\n    title: \\\"MiniPLM: Knowledge Distillation for Pre-Training Language Models\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Yuxian Gu, Hao Zhou, Fandong Meng, Jie Zhou, Minlie Huang\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"ICLR 2025\\\"\\n    year: 2025\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.17215\\\"\\n    category: \\\"method-component\\\"\\n    relevance: \\\"离线蒸馏框架，通过调整训练数据分布传递教师知识，跨模型族蒸馏\\\"\\n    key_findings: \\\"Difference Sampling 基于教师/参考模型输出概率差异调整训练语料；9 个下游任务提升；减少预训练计算量\\\"\\n    our_advantage: \\\"MiniPLM 是通用预训练蒸馏，DTD 是任务特异的 trace 过滤蒸馏；可借鉴其 Difference Sampling 思想\\\"\\n\\n  - id: \\\"kd_code_2025\\\"\\n    title: \\\"An Empirical Study of Knowledge Distillation for Code Understanding Tasks\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Code KD study authors\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"ICSE 2026\\\"\\n    year: 2025\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.15423\\\"\\n    category: \\\"method-component\\\"\\n    relevance: \\\"系统评估多种 KD 方法在代码理解任务上的表现，为 DTD 的蒸馏策略选择提供依据\\\"\\n    key_findings: \\\"首次系统比较多种 KD 方法在代码理解任务（漏洞检测/克隆检测等）上的效果\\\"\\n    our_advantage: \\\"DTD 可参考其 KD 方法选择建议，但 DTD 的蒸馏目标是 trace 结构过滤而非通用代码理解\\\"\\n\\n  # ============================================================\\n  # Evaluation — benchmarks and evaluation methodology\\n  # ============================================================\\n\\n  - A Comprehensive Study on Execution Trace-Based Information for Code Large Language Models\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Jian Wang, Xiaofei Xie, Qiang Hu, Shangqing Liu, Yi Li\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"Findings of EMNLP 2025\\\"\\n    year: 2025\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.11686\\\"\\n    category: \\\"direct-competitor\\\"\\n    relevance: \\\"DTD 直接要修正的目标论文——其负面结论 (trace utility limited) 源于行级未过滤 trace\\\"\\n    key_findings: \\\"语义信息（execution trace）对 Code LLM 的 SFT 和推理帮助有限；在 6 组 dataset/model 配置中仅 2 组有正向效果\\\"\\n    our_advantage: \\\"DTD 用结构感知分段（函数子树/循环组/异常链）替代原始行级 trace，降低噪声，直接回应其负面结论\\\"\\n\\n  - id: \\\"bouzenia2023tracefixer\\\"\\n    title: \\\"TraceFixer: Execution Trace-Driven Program Repair\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Islem Bouzenia, Yangruibo Ding, Kexin Pei, Baishakhi Ray, Michael Pradel\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"arXiv preprint\\\"\\n    year: 2023\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2304.12743\\\"\\n    category: \\\"direct-competitor\\\"\\n    relevance: \\\"最早将 execution trace 直接融入神经修复模型的工作，trace 表示为局部变量值序列\\\"\\n    key_findings: \\\"在 Python 单行 bug 上 top-1 修复率 57-63%，trace 带来 13-20% 提升；在 20 个真实 bug 中修复 10 个\\\"\\n    our_advantage: \\\"TraceFixer 用扁平变量快照，DTD 用层次化结构分段；TraceFixer 限于单行 bug，DTD 处理多行复杂修复\\\"\\n\\n  - id: \\\"tracerepair2026\\\"\\n    title: \\\"Runtime Execution Traces Guided Automated Program Repair with Multi-Agent Debate\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"TraceRepair authors\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"arXiv preprint\\\"\\n    year: 2026\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02647\\\"\\n    category: \\\"direct-competitor\\\"\\n    relevance: \\\"最新的 trace-guided APR，用 multi-agent debate 将 trace 作为逻辑过滤器\\\"\\n    key_findings: \\\"Probe Agent 注入日志捕获变量快照；多 agent 辩论用 trace 证据过滤幻觉补丁；Defects4J 上修复 392 个 bug\\\"\\n    our_advantage: \\\"TraceRepair 依赖大 LLM agent 推理链，DTD 训练专用小模型做 trace 过滤，成本更低且可离线使用\\\"\\n\\n  - id: \\\"huang2025dynafix\\\"\\n    title: \\\"DynaFix: Iterative Automated Program Repair Driven by Execution-Level Dynamic Information\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Zhili Huang, Ling Xu, Chao Liu, Weifeng Sun, Xu Zhang, Yan Lei, Meng Yan, Hongyu Zhang\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"arXiv preprint\\\"\\n    year: 2025\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.24635\\\"\\n    category: \\\"direct-competitor\\\"\\n    relevance: \\\"迭代式利用运行时状态（变量值/分支路径/调用栈）驱动修复的闭环框架\\\"\\n    key_findings: \\\"用 ByteTrace 工具收集细粒度执行状态；Defects4J-Trans 上比 SOTA 提升 15.6-32.1%；token 消耗降低 60-70%\\\"\\n    our_advantage: \\\"DynaFix 每轮全量收集 trace 再让 LLM 推理，DTD 预先用小模型做结构化过滤，减少 LLM 输入噪声\\\"\\n\\n  - Let me review the new entries:

1. **SWEzze (jia2026swezze)**: Marked as "method-component". OCD + Qwen3-Reranker-0.6B for static code compression. Correctly positioned — operates on static code, not traces. ✓

2. **SWE-Pruner (wang2026swepruner)**: Marked as "method-component". CRF-based code pruning. Correctly positioned — static code only. ✓
   
3. **Squeez (kovacs2026squeez)**: Marked as "direct-competitor". It does line-level tool output pruning with strong metrics (92% token removal, 0.86 recall) using LoRA fine-tuned Qwen 3.5 2B. The key question is whether its coverage of 27 tool types includes execution traces — if it handles trace output alongside other tool outputs, then the direct-competitor classification makes sense.

4. **TACO (ren2026taco)**: Marked as "method-component". This is terminal observation compression using rules, which is correctly positioned as a training-free approach that only handles terminal output. ✓

5. **CodeComp (chen2026codecomp)**: Marked as "method-component". It does KV cache compression using Code Property Graph, operating at the cache level rather than on trace text itself. The finding that structure-aware approaches outperform structure-agnostic ones supports DTD's positioning. ✓

Looking across these papers, the distinction for DTD's novelty is becoming clearer: SWEzze and SWE-Pruner focus on static code, TACO handles terminal output only, and Squeez does flat line-level pruning of tool outputs — but DTD's structural segmentation of execution traces appears to be the novel angle.

The key refinement is that DTD's claim should be framed as "first structure-aware trace compression" rather than just "first trace compression," since Squeez might already handle trace-like outputs but without leveraging their hierarchical structure. I'm also noticing some entries have incomplete author attributions that should be cleaned up.

**格式硬约束**:
- 整条回复**只能**是 &lt;handoff>...&lt;/handoff> 标签包裹

**必须覆盖以下 5 个 section（缺一不可，无内容写 N/A）**:

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
  本项目特定的不可违反约束（GPU 设备号、服务器名、账号、路径限制等）。
  写本项目特定的，不要抄 CLAUDE.md 通用 L1 红线。
  下一个"你"第一眼看到的就是这里，写错了会被 Director CRITICAL。

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
  - 服务器名 + 工作目录 + Python 路径 + conda env
  - 模型路径 / 数据集路径 / wandb config / HF cache
  - 活跃进程：PID + tmux session + cron 任务 ID
  写一次就稳定，下一个"你"不用再 SSH 探查。

### 3. 📊 当前状态
  - 你刚做完什么 / 正在做什么
  - registry 里 RUNNING/PREPARING 的实验列表 + 一句话摘要
  - 等待中的 Worker 回调 / Cron / Director 决策

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
  格式 "条件 X 满足 → 动作 Y"。例如:
  - "Falcon-H1R 下载完成 → sed 修 MODEL_PATH → dry-run → 提交"
  - "pilot_011 出 GC4 结果 → ≥2pp 转架构 narrative，<2pp 写 null-result"

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
  - 项目核心结论 / 决策链（D001..）
  - 下一个"你"最容易判断错的事 / 容易踩的坑

**写作原则**：
- 第 1 节必须是项目特定的（不是通用 L1 红线）
- 第 2 节让下一轮 BOOT 后能立即开工，避免重新 SSH 探查
- 简洁 > 完整：每节 5-10 行足够，长 handoff 不等于好 handoff
